					}
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 3
					add_building_construction = {
						type = dockyard
						level = 3
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}

			navy_experience = 50

			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {

							is_coastal = yes
						}
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = wuw_HUN_renew_the_shipping_industry_tt
			}
			else = {
				random_owned_controlled_state = {
					prioritize = { 2830 } # was vanilla 103 (Croatia)
					limit = {
						is_coastal = yes
						free_building_slots = {
							building = dockyard
							size > 2
							include_locked = yes
						}
					}
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 3
					add_building_construction = {
						type = dockyard
						level = 3
						instant_build = yes
					}
					set_state_flag = HUN_dockyards_flag
				}
			}
		}
